# Feed parity matrix

Generated by `scripts/feed_corpus/parity_matrix.py` from the feed corpus
(`src-tauri/tests/fixtures/feed_corpus`); do not edit by hand. A cell lists the
corpus cases covering that broker, mode and depth, or its order updates, with
the worst result among them; what the web offers is each broker's
`broker.json`. A result holds when the case's test passes in CI. 1.0.7 ships
when every cell is "matches web", "approved deviation" or "not offered by the web".

Cases: 379. Open gaps: 0. Deviations pending approval: 852.

| arrow | `ltp_mode` | `close` | desktop-decision | yes | Market-state rule (lead 2026-11-09: the previous close overlays in the snapshot). A 13-byte LTP packet carries no previous close. The bridge keeps the instrument's last known close within one connection (feed/bridge/snapshot.rs, audit C03) and works the change out again from the new price, so the frame carries the close and change the 17-byte packet brought; the web's frame from a 13-byte packet has none (the Arrow adapter keeps no snapshot). Nothing is invented: every value is the broker's. |
| arrow | `ltp_mode` | `prev_close` | desktop-decision | yes | Market-state rule (lead 2026-11-09: the previous close overlays in the snapshot). A 13-byte LTP packet carries no previous close. Nothing is invented: every value is the broker's. |

| arrow | `order_stop_loss_margin` | `product` | web-defect | yes | Arrow's M is NRML everywhere the web maps it for placing and for its order book (transform_data.py:28, 35); the web's order stream alone calls it MIS, so an NRML order's updates name the wrong product. | compositedge | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | no | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| fivepaisa | `depth_before_quote` | `ltp` | desktop-decision | yes | A depth frame keeps ltp and timestamp before the instrument's first quote, for every broker (1.0.6 decision: the SDK's Depth callback reads them); the 5paisa web adapter leaves ltp out of a MarketDepthService frame that comes before any MarketFeedV3 frame (audit RB-04). The other quote fields are left out on both. |
| fivepaisa | `index_quote` | `last_quantity` | web-defect | yes | 5paisa fills an index's traded quantity and book totals with 0 (an index has no trades and no book); the web passes the zeros on (_quote_fields feed.get("LastQty", 0), feed.get("TBidQ", 0)), the desktop's index frame leaves them out. Web sends zero; docs say leave out. |
| fivepaisa | `index_quote` | `last_trade_quantity` | web-defect | yes | 5paisa fills an index's traded quantity and book totals with 0 (an index has no trades and no book); the web passes the zeros on (_quote_fields feed.get("LastQty", 0), feed.get("TBidQ", 0)), the desktop's index frame leaves them out. Web sends zero; docs say leave out. |
| fivepaisa | `index_quote` | `average_price` | web-defect | yes | 5paisa sends an index's average price as 0, a price it does not have; the web passes the 0 on (_quote_fields feed.get("AvgRate", 0)), the desktop leaves out a price 0 with nothing held, as for every instrument. An invented zero on the web. |
| fivepaisa | `index_quote` | `total_buy_quantity` | web-defect | yes | 5paisa fills an index's traded quantity and book totals with 0 (an index has no trades and no book); the web passes the zeros on (_quote_fields feed.get("LastQty", 0), feed.get("TBidQ", 0)), the desktop's index frame leaves them out. Web sends zero; docs say leave out. |

An invented zero on the web; the quantities (0, sent) match. |
| fivepaisa | `index_quote` | `ask_price` | web-defect | yes | 5paisa sends an index's best bid and ask price as 0, prices it does not have; the web passes the 0 on (_normalize_market_data feed.get("BidRate", 0)), the desktop leaves out a price 0 with nothing held. | fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `frame` | web-defect | yes | The web's 5paisa book (transform_order_data) has no filled_quantity, so the poller's (order_status, filled_quantity) never changes on a partial fill and the web publishes nothing for it; the desktop reads TradedQty and publishes the fill. |
| fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `filled_quantity` | web-defect | yes | The web sends 0 for every 5paisa order (its book has no filled_quantity), a completed one included; the desktop sends TradedQty. |
| fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `pending_quantity` | web-defect | yes | The web sends 0 for every 5paisa order (its book has no pending_quantity); the desktop sends PendingQty. |
| fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `average_price` | web-defect | yes | The web sends 0.0 for every 5paisa order (its book has no average_price); the desktop sends AveragePrice. |
| fivepaisa | `order_book_polling` | `rejection_reason` | web-defect | yes | The web's 5paisa book carries 5paisa's Reason as reason, and the poller reads rejection_reason, so a rejected order goes out with an empty reason; the desktop sends 5paisa's RMS text. Lost data on the web. |
| fivepaisa | `reconnect_after_held_prices` | `open` | web-defect | yes | The web keeps its snapshot across a reconnect (fivepaisa_adapter.py _on_open and _on_close leave last_snapshot), so a frame 5paisa sends after it with a price of 0 goes out with the pre-outage price as current; the desktop starts each connection afresh and leaves a price it does not have out (lead decision: restarting the merge after a reconnect is a desktop improvement). |

| fivepaisa | `reconnect_after_held_prices` | `missing-frame` | web-defect | yes | After the reconnect 5paisa's first frame reports 0 for every price: the web publishes a quote with its pre-outage prices as current (its last_snapshot survives the reconnect); the desktop starts the new connection afresh and publishes nothing without a traded price (lead decision: restarting the merge after a reconnect is a desktop improvement). |

| fivepaisaxts | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | no | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| fivepaisaxts | `text_1105` | `frame` | web-defect | no | The web passes a 1105 text event to its adapter without a MessageCode, and the adapter drops it as an unknown code; the desktop decodes the touchline its codes 110-119 carry (audit appendix, inherited item 4). |
| fivepaisaxts | `unsubscribe_keeps_the_others` | `ltt` | web-defect | no | The web sends XTS's LastTradedTime as it comes, seconds since 1980-01-01 IST (1478686530 is 2026-11-09 10:15:30 IST): a wrong unit, since feed timestamps are Unix epoch milliseconds (docs/prompt/websockets-format.md). | flattrade | `depth_future_oi` | `last_trade_time` | web-defect | no | The web's Flattrade adapter merges every raw key into its scrip cache, so a packet that carries a new price and no time still goes out with Noren's old trade time ltt: a value of an earlier packet presented as this one's. The desktop sends a packet's times only from that packet, and a change worked out from the new price (lead decision, review-fixes.md: a per-packet time never carries over). |
| flattrade | `depth_future_oi` | `oi` | web-defect | no | Noren's toi is the total open interest (Shoonya's adapter names it open_interest); the web's Flattrade adapter sends it only as total_traded_value, a turnover name, so a client reading oi loses it. The desktop sends total_traded_value as the web does and keeps oi (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |
| flattrade | `frame_without_exchange` | `total_buy_quantity` | web-defect | no | Noren's touchline carries the book totals (tbq, tsq), and the web's Flattrade Quote dict drops them: lost data. The desktop keeps them (lead decision: data a web adapter drops from a mode is kept). |
| flattrade | `frame_without_exchange` | `total_sell_quantity` | web-defect | no | Noren's touchline carries the book totals (tbq, tsq), and the web's Flattrade Quote dict drops them: lost data. The desktop keeps them (lead decision: data a web adapter drops from a mode is kept). |
| flattrade | `index_ltp_and_quote` | `average_price` | web-defect | no | Noren's index touchline carries no average price; the web's Flattrade adapter sends its safe_float/safe_int default (0) for a field the stream did not carry; the docs leave such a field out (web openalgo#2376, docs/api/websocket-streaming/quote.md), and so does the desktop. |

| iifl | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | no | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| iifl | `text_1105` | `frame` | web-defect | no | The web drops a 1105 text event: its client compares the stored string instrument id with an int, so no 1105 event passes its subscription filter, and its adapter drops a message without MessageCode as an unknown code. The desktop decodes the touchline its codes 110-119 carry (audit appendix, inherited item 4). |
| iifl | `unsubscribe_keeps_the_others` | `ltt` | web-defect | no | The web sends XTS's LastTradedTime as it comes, seconds since 1980-01-01 IST (1478686530 is 2026-11-09 10:15:30 IST): a wrong unit, since feed timestamps are Unix epoch milliseconds (docs/prompt/websockets-format.md). | iifl | `unsubscribe_keeps_the_others` | `frame` | web-defect | no | This web adapter disconnects the whole socket after any unsubscribe (self.disconnect() in unsubscribe), so every other symbol stops streaming; the desktop removes only the instrument let go (audit appendix, inherited item 3). |
| iiflcapital | `full_depth` | `ltt` | web-defect | yes | The web passes IIFL's trade time on in epoch seconds; WebSocket times are epoch milliseconds (docs/prompt/websockets-format.md, CLAUDE.md), so a client reading ltt as ms on the web lands in January 1970. The desktop sends milliseconds. |
| iiflcapital | `full_depth` | `low` | web-defect | yes | The web multiplies the packet's integer price by 1/divisor, which is inexact in binary (148510 x 0.01 = 1485.1000000000001); the desktop divides by the divisor and sends the price the exchange quoted. |
| iiflcapital | `full_depth` | `ask` | web-defect | yes | The web multiplies the packet's integer price by 1/divisor, which is inexact in binary (148510 x 0.01 = 1485.1000000000001); the desktop divides by the divisor and sends the price the exchange quoted. |

| jainamxts | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | no | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| jainamxts | `oi_event_1510_kept` | `missing-frame` | web-defect | no | Jainam's adapter publishes the 1510 open-interest event and the 1505 candle to every held mode as market frames: a Depth frame with an LTP of 0 for each, the 1510's carrying only its OI right, the 1505's the one-minute bar's open, high, low and close as the day's. An LTP of 0 is a price nobody traded (rules: no zero LTP), so the desktop sends no frame for a message without a trade; the 1510's OI goes with the next priced message (feed/bridge.rs convert). |

| jainamxts | `quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | no | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. | kotak | `depth_book_replaced` | `depth` | web-defect | yes | The web pads each side of an SFeed book to five rows of price 0, quantity 0 (sfeed_websocket.py::_depth_rows): levels the exchange does not have, with zero prices. The desktop sends the book's real levels only (lead decision 9 Nov: zero-padded levels -> the real ones, web defect). |
| kotak | `index_depth` | `volume` | web-defect | yes | An SFeed index packet carries no volume; the web's index normaliser writes volume 0 (sfeed_websocket.py::_to_index) and publishes it, a zero the exchange never sent. The desktop leaves it out (web openalgo#2376). |
| kotak | `index_depth` | `totalbuyqty` | web-defect | yes | An index has no book; the web sends its book totals as 0 under the Kotak name (extras.get(..., 0)), numbers the exchange never sent. The desktop leaves them out. |
| kotak | `index_depth` | `totalsellqty` | web-defect | yes | An index has no book; the web sends its book totals as 0 under the Kotak name (extras.get(..., 0)), numbers the exchange never sent. The desktop leaves them out. |
| kotak | `index_two_names_one_feed` | `volume` | web-defect | yes | An SFeed index packet carries no volume; the web's index normaliser writes volume 0 (sfeed_websocket.py::_to_index) and publishes it, a zero the exchange never sent. The desktop leaves it out (web openalgo#2376). |
| kotak | `reconnect_clears_the_book` | `depth` | web-defect | yes | The web pads each side of an SFeed book to five rows of price 0, quantity 0 (sfeed_websocket.py::_depth_rows): levels the exchange does not have, with zero prices. | pocketful | `two_clients_ltp_and_quote` | `frame` | web-defect | yes | With an LTP and a Quote subscriber on one instrument, the web's _find_subscription returns the first subscription (the LTP one) for every packet, so the detailed packet goes out on the LTP topic with data.mode 1 and the Quote subscriber gets nothing at all (PF-03). | rmoney | `unsubscribe_keeps_the_others` | `frame` | web-defect | no | This web adapter disconnects the whole socket after any unsubscribe (self.disconnect() in unsubscribe), so every other symbol stops streaming; the desktop removes only the instrument let go (audit appendix, inherited item 3). |
| rupeezy | `future_ltp_and_depth` | `close` | desktop-decision | yes | Market-state rule (lead 2026-11-09: the previous close overlays in the snapshot). An ltp packet carries no previous close. The bridge keeps the instrument's last known close within one connection (feed/bridge/snapshot.rs, audit C03) and works the change out again from the new price, so the LTP frame carries the close and change the full packet brought; the web's frame from an ltp packet has none (the Rupeezy adapter keeps no snapshot). Every value is the broker's. |
| rupeezy | `future_ltp_and_depth` | `prev_close` | desktop-decision | yes | Market-state rule (lead 2026-11-09: the previous close overlays in the snapshot). An ltp packet carries no previous close. The bridge keeps the instrument's last known close within one connection (feed/bridge/snapshot.rs, audit C03) and works the change out again from the new price, so the LTP frame carries the close and change the full packet brought; the web's frame from an ltp packet has none (the Rupeezy adapter keeps no snapshot). Every value is the broker's. |

The bridge keeps the instrument's last known close within one connection (feed/bridge/snapshot.rs, audit C03) and works the change out again from the new price, so the LTP frame carries the close and change the full packet brought; the web's frame from an ltp packet has none (the Rupeezy adapter keeps no snapshot). Every value is the broker's. |

Every value is the broker's. |
| rupeezy | `future_ltp_and_depth` | `ltt` | web-defect | yes | The web sends null; the docs say leave it out (lead decision 2026-11-09, 4(c)): an ltp packet, or an ohlcv packet before the first trade, carries no trade time, so the desktop's frame has no ltt, where the web sends ltt null. |

The desktop keeps every level Sahi sent, up to five. |

| tradejini | `currency_quote_divisor` | `ltt` | web-defect | yes | Tradejini sends the trade time in epoch seconds; the web formats it with the server's local time zone into a text such as "2025-11-05 14:25:31" (nxtradstream.py datefmt) and sends that as ltt, where feed timestamps are epoch milliseconds (docs/prompt/websockets-format.md). The desktop sends epoch ms, and leaves ltt out when Tradejini sent no trade time, where the web sends null (Quote, Depth) or "" (LTP). |

| tradejini | `ltp_mode` | `ltt` | web-defect | yes | Tradejini sends the trade time in epoch seconds; the web formats it with the server's local time zone into a text such as "2025-11-05 14:25:31" (nxtradstream.py datefmt) and sends that as ltt, where feed timestamps are epoch milliseconds (docs/prompt/websockets-format.md). The desktop sends epoch ms, and leaves ltt out when Tradejini sent no trade time, where the web sends null (Quote, Depth) or "" (LTP). |

| wisdom | `index_quote_touchline` | `average_price` | web-defect | no | With the 1501 quote nested in Touchline (the shape of this case) this web adapter reads 1501 at the message root ('data is at root level', :775) and sends average_price as 0: a wrong value, lost data. The desktop reads Touchline when the message has one, else the root, so it is right whichever shape XTS sends (quote_root and index_quote_root pin the root shape, which matches). Which shape a real 1501-json-full has is for the maintainer's market-hours check; this deviation holds when XTS nests the quote (lead ruling 2026-11-09). |

| zebu | `depth_future_oi` | `last_trade_time` | web-defect | no | The web's Zebu adapter merges every raw key into its scrip cache, so a packet that carries a new price and no time still goes out with Noren's old trade time ltt: a value of an earlier packet presented as this one's. | zebu | `depth_future_oi` | `oi` | web-defect | no | Noren's toi is the total open interest (Shoonya's adapter names it open_interest); the web's Zebu adapter sends it only as total_traded_value, a turnover name, so a client reading oi loses it.
